# Master Reference Bridge Atlas — 2026-05-06

## Summary

This is the interconnectivity pass for the master reference list. It treats the SAFE’s 1004 annotated bibliography entries as living research infrastructure and links each citation to the parts of the vault that already carry that theme, method, or lineage.

## Bridge Rule

- Each entry is placed under one primary theme section.
- Each entry gets one or more links to the brain surfaces that hold its likely use.
- Secondary tag or title matches add cross-links so a citation can travel across multiple vault clusters.

## Tier Definitions

- **AI Governance, Policy, and Regulation** — Work on AI systems, regulation, accountability, model behavior, governance controls, and policy response.
- **Governance and Institutions** — Institutional governance, legitimacy, oversight, rule systems, and how power is organized through structures.
- **Power, Security, and Political Control** — Security, coercion, surveillance, consent, boundary enforcement, and political control.
- **Research Methods** — Research design, qualitative method, evidence handling, analysis practice, and methodological reflection.
- **Gender, Queer, and Embodiment Studies** — Gender, queer theory, embodiment, identity, intersectionality, and related social formation.
- **Ritual, Religion, and Spirituality** — Religion, ritual, magic, spirituality, sacred practice, and institutionalized symbolic systems.
- **Media and Cultural Analysis** — Media studies, pop culture, narrative production, fan text, and cultural interpretation.
- **Posthumanism, Ecology, and the Anthropocene** — Posthumanism, ecology, more-than-human systems, environment, and the Anthropocene.
- **Martin — Public / Professional Surface** — Public-facing Martin materials: identity, consulting, skills, and the external professional surface.
- **Martin — Scholarly Publication Surface** — Martin Lepage's scholarly publication surface: papers, chapters, and academic references.
- **Martin — Creative / Compositional Surface** — Creative and compositional Martin materials: novels, monographs, fiction, and the writing surface.
- **Tier 0 — General Theory or Context Building** — Tier 0 bridge material: broad theory, context-setting, and references that bridge across multiple clusters without a narrower home.
